861 entries, ArchitectureReviewer to article-linker. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

([zarfld/IntelAvbFilter](https://github.com/zarfld/IntelAvbFilter/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/architecture-strategist.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **ArchitectUX**: Technical UX architect who provides solid CSS/layout systems, IA, and implementation guidance so developers ship fast and consistently. ([Crumbgrabber/llm_system_template_agents_skills_patterns_tools_prompts](https://github.com/Crumbgrabber/llm_system_template_agents_skills_patterns_tools_prompts/blob/HEAD/agents/design-ux-architect.md) · ★3)
- **architekt**: Software-Architekt. Legt Tech-Stack, Projektstruktur, Datenmodell, Schnittstellen und Hosting fest. Nach dem PL, vor Design und Umsetzung einsetzen. ([IT-Money97/KI-Agent-Team](https://github.com/IT-Money97/KI-Agent-Team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/architekt.md))
- **architekt**: Hält die Gesamtstruktur. Bewertet Vorhaben in drei Stufen mit Kosten, beantwortet Strukturfragen des Planers, macht die Bestandsaufnahme in bestehenden Projekten und sucht in der Wochenrunde nach Drift und sichtet die Pflegeliste. Aufruf mit "Anlass: bewertung | strukturfrage | bestandsaufnahme | wochenrunde". ([xXExceptionXx/keel](https://github.com/xXExceptionXx/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/architekt.md))
- **Architekt aplikacji LLM**: Inżynier AI buduje aplikacje oparte o duże modele jezykowe: systemy RAG, asystentów, pipeline promptów i integracje z API modeli. Nie trenuje modeli od zera - składa działające systemy z gotowych komponentów i pilnuje trzech rzeczy naraz: kosztu, latencji i jakości. Każde twierdzenie o jakości popiera ewaluacja, nie intuicja. ([TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture](https://github.com/TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture/blob/HEAD/agents/ai_engineer.md) · ★111)
- **Architekt Dokumentow Kariery**: Architekt Dokumentow Kariery buduje i poprawia CV, listy motywacyjne oraz sekcje profilu LinkedIn. Dziala na REALNYCH danych kariery uzytkownika - nigdy nie zmysla osiagniec, dat ani liczb (zasada zero-fabrication). Dopasowuje dokument do konkretnej oferty (ATS-aware) i moze reuzywac profil glosu do tonu listu/LinkedIn. Dziala po polsku i angielsku. ([TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture](https://github.com/TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture/blob/HEAD/agents/career_document_builder.md) · ★111)
- **Architekt kieszonkowy**: Deweloper Mobilny buduje aplikacje na iOS i Android - natywnie (Swift/Kotlin) albo cross-platform (React Native/Flutter). Zajmuje się ekranami, nawigacja, stanem i integracjami z funkcjami urządzenia (kamera, GPS, push, biometria), z zalozeniem offline-first: aplikacja działa bez zasięgu, a dane synchronizują się, gdy siec wroci. Pilnuje wytycznych platform, budżetu baterii i przygotowania do review sklepu. ([TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture](https://github.com/TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture/blob/HEAD/agents/mobile_developer.md) · ★111)
- **Architekt wnetrz aplikacji**: Designer to agent implementacji wizualnej w warstwie BUILD. Otrzymuje raporty Researcher UX i przeksztalca je w działający CSS, design tokeny, palety kolorów, typografie i animacje. Jest mostem między inspiracja wizualna a kodem produkcyjnym. ([TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture](https://github.com/TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture/blob/HEAD/agents/designer.md) · ★111)

Read-only; never edits, writes or spawns. ([frandev94/strategium](https://github.com/frandev94/strategium/blob/HEAD/agents/archmagos.md))
- **archman**: Используй этого агента, когда нужно спроектировать или проаудитить структуру проекта, границы модулей, контракты данных или подготовить execution-план до имплементации. Используй PROACTIVELY всегда, когда задача затрагивает несколько модулей (например, core/dsp ↔ core/ffi ↔ apps/mobile), при введении нового публичного API или при предложении UI-first / contract-skipping подхода. По умолчанию read-only. ([filatelist-hitech/TrackScope](https://github.com/filatelist-hitech/TrackScope/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/archman.md))
- **archmantic-architect**: Solution architect for Archmantic. Use for architecture decisions, IR/model design, evaluating technical trade-offs, and keeping docs/ARCHITECTURE.md coherent. Guards the model-first design and the provenance invariant. ([mgionas/Archmantic](https://github.com/mgionas/Archmantic/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/archmantic-architect.md))
- **archmantic-explorer**: Read-only codebase explorer that answers architecture / onboarding / "how does X work" / "what depends on Y" / "what's the data model or API surface" questions by querying the Archmantic model (MCP) FIRST, reading files only as fallback. Delegate codebase-understanding and research tasks here — especially in multi-agent runs — to get grounded answers for far fewer tokens. Requires the repo to have .archmantic/model.json. ([mgionas/Archmantic](https://github.com/mgionas/Archmantic/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/archmantic-explorer.md))
- **archmap**: Лёгкая детерминированная TS/JS architecture/dependency map: architecture.json и interactive HTML. Полный multi-language пакет и review принадлежат principal-architect. ([Vitammiin/agent-vorcl-flow](https://github.com/Vitammiin/agent-vorcl-flow/blob/HEAD/agents/archmap.md) · ★2)
- **archmap-explorer**: Reads one area's prescan summary, confirms it against the code, adds what prescan cannot see, writes one size-capped JSON fragment of nodes, edges, entities and access rows with evidence to the fragment path it is given, and replies with three lines. Use it once per area during /archmap:build and /archmap:update, in parallel, never on the whole repo. ([AleksandarBisevac/claude-archmap](https://github.com/AleksandarBisevac/claude-archmap/blob/HEAD/agents/archmap-explorer.md))
- **archmap-explorer**: Thoroughly explores a codebase and returns modules, internal dependencies, tiers, and pipeline flow for /archmap:generate. ([juxstin1/archmap-plugin](https://github.com/juxstin1/archmap-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/archmap-explorer.md))
- **archmap-repair-agent**: Targeted codebase re-exploration for map repair. Accepts suspected stale modules and reports what changed vs. existing map data. ([juxstin1/archmap-plugin](https://github.com/juxstin1/archmap-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/archmap-repair-agent.md))
- **archmap-reviewer**: Reviews the architecture model for architecture-level issues - security, data, reliability, performance, design and docs - and writes them as an issues list anchored to model ids to the reviewer path its brief names, replying in three lines. Use it only when /archmap:build or /archmap:issues runs with --issues. ([AleksandarBisevac/claude-archmap](https://github.com/AleksandarBisevac/claude-archmap/blob/HEAD/agents/archmap-reviewer.md))
- **archmap-synthesizer**: Reads the merge digest (node ids, edges, entities, access rows and pre-drafted sequences) and writes the narrative layer - 3-7 sequences and the views - as one JSON object to the synth path its brief names, replying in three lines. Use it after merge in /archmap:build, and only for touched sequences and views in /archmap:update. ([AleksandarBisevac/claude-archmap](https://github.com/AleksandarBisevac/claude-archmap/blob/HEAD/agents/archmap-synthesizer.md))

- **archsutra-atlas**: Use this agent when the question is about business capabilities, bounded contexts, team and system ownership, governance, or build-versus-buy across several systems. Typical triggers include "where should the boundary between these services be", "map our capabilities to systems", and "should we build or buy this". Do not use it for code-level or infrastructure details. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/atlas.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-blueprint**: Use this agent when architecture documents or diagrams are needed or out of date. Typical triggers include "draw the C4 diagrams", "write the HLD and LLD", and "record this decision as an ADR". Do not use it to invent structure that is not in the code or requirements. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/blueprint.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-cloud**: Use this agent when the question is about deployment topology, networking, identity, containers or Kubernetes, infrastructure as code, disaster recovery, or regions. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/cloud.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-data**: Use this agent when the question concerns data models, ownership, consistency, transactions, retention, or migrations. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/data.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-forge**: Use this agent when a design or audit must become developer-ready work: refactoring plans, strangler migrations, and task breakdowns. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/forge.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-ledger**: Use this agent when cost, budget, vendor lock-in, or operational burden drives an architecture decision. - **archsutra-neural**: Use this agent when a system uses language models, retrieval, agents, tool calling, MCP or similar protocols. - **archsutra-nexus**: Use this agent when the question concerns service-to-service or external integration: REST, GraphQL, gRPC, events, queues, webhooks, contracts, or versioning. - **archsutra-prime**: Use this agent when a user asks for a software architecture design, audit, review, modernization plan or a threat model and no single specialist is obviously right. - **archsutra-scale**: Use this agent when the concern is performance, capacity, availability, observability, or fault tolerance. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/scale.md) · ★4)

- **armature-inventor**: Frontier-robotics researcher — recent papers, novel mechanisms, unusual actuators and materials, filtered against the project's physics into an innovation brief at docs/research/. Dispatch when a design is stuck or conventional, or a trade study's requirements are unusually hard. One idea family per run (mechanism, actuation, material, sensing, manufacturing method), two or three runs at a time, each prompt carrying the design tension in one sentence, the constraint numbers, and what is already ruled out. ([rioharper/armature](https://github.com/rioharper/armature/blob/HEAD/agents/inventor.md))
- **armature-librarian**: Datasheet and OTS-model hunter for robotics parts — finds the document, verifies the exact part number, caches it with provenance in docs/datasheets/ and cad/ots-parts/, and stages its numbers as ledger entries. Dispatch when a decision needs a datasheet number, a list price, or a vendor CAD model the project lacks. Give the exact P/N (cached this run), or a description plus the numbers the decision needs (returned as a candidate for the user to confirm). One part or one slice of a field per run, two or three runs at a time. ([rioharper/armature](https://github.com/rioharper/armature/blob/HEAD/agents/librarian.md))
- **armature-red-team**: Adversarial reviewer for robotics artifacts (specs, derivations, plans, BOMs, part definitions), fresh context by construction — findings report to docs/reviews/, each fix routed to its owner. Dispatch it for every review of an artifact the user asks for; whenever an artifact is drafted or finished; and always before CAD hours or purchases. Commit first, then pass the commit, the artifact paths, the decision the review gates, any risk already accepted, and the report path and format the user fixed. A recheck passes the review, the finding ids and the fix diff. ([rioharper/armature](https://github.com/rioharper/armature/blob/HEAD/agents/red-team.md))
- **Armin**: Smart infrastructure — cost-effective solutions, clever architecture, resource-efficient design patterns ([tmcleod3/voidforge](https://github.com/tmcleod3/voidforge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/armin-clever.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **armory-catalog-auditor**: Audits the health of the whole Armory catalog after a nightly refresh or before a release. It reports; it does not change data. ([naman10parikh/armory](https://github.com/naman10parikh/armory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/armory-catalog-auditor.md))
- **armory-curator**: Reviews candidate components before they enter Armory, one by one or a whole incoming/ batch. ([naman10parikh/armory](https://github.com/naman10parikh/armory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/armory-curator.md))

- **arquitecto**: Redacta el plan de implementación de una feature o cambio no trivial como contrato (objetivo, decisiones, cambios por fichero, contrato, migraciones, definición de hecho, tareas), explorando el código y priorizando reutilizar lo existente. No escribe código ni revisa planes ajenos (eso es dev:revisor-plan). ([QudeCode/ai-bank](https://github.com/QudeCode/ai-bank/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/agents/arquitecto.md))
- **arquitecto**: Usar al inicio de una funcionalidad nueva, al cambiar límites entre módulos, al agregar una dependencia externa o cuando haya que decidir entre dos enfoques. Diseña y documenta decisiones (ADR) sin escribir código de producción. ([segurolotengopy/ManejoQRSimple](https://github.com/segurolotengopy/ManejoQRSimple/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arquitecto.md))
- **arquitecto**: Propone la ESTRUCTURA antes de escribir código -- dónde va una pieza nueva, cómo se conectan dos, dónde vive un artefacto. Aplica las 19 reglas de docs/reglas-de-diseno.md entrando por su tabla de disparadores, da 2-3 opciones con su coste y recomienda una. Úsalo ante cualquier decisión de estructura, interfaz o ubicación. ([stalinbeltran/telegram-coordinator](https://github.com/stalinbeltran/telegram-coordinator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arquitecto.md))
- **arquitecto--connex**: Diseña la arquitectura del proyecto CONNEX con criterio de Principal Architect. Úsalo para planear features nuevas, decidir cómo organizar código nuevo, resolver trade-offs técnicos de alto nivel, o revisar si la estructura actual escala correctamente. Ejemplos: '¿Cómo estructuro el módulo de cola de mensajes para WhatsApp?', '¿Dónde va la lógica de refresh token?', 'Diseña la arquitectura de los workflows de automatización', '¿Cómo escalamos a multi-tenant sin reescribir todo?'. ([JoseMsD21/CONNEX](https://github.com/JoseMsD21/CONNEX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arquitecto--connex.md))
- **arquitecto-agentes**: Estudia el workflow real de un repositorio (tareas que se repiten, flujos, comandos, agentes y skills existentes, historial de git) y propone qué agentes, skills y workflows del banco ai-bank usar, cuáles faltan y qué debe ir en el contexto específico del proyecto. Úsalo al adoptar el banco en un proyecto nuevo o al rediseñar el reparto de trabajo. Solo propone; no crea ni edita archivos. ([QudeCode/ai-bank](https://github.com/QudeCode/ai-bank/blob/HEAD/plugins/meta/agents/arquitecto-agentes.md))
- **arquitecto-backend**: Diseña APIs, base de datos y arquitectura sin escribir código. Backend architect for API design, database schemas, microservice boundaries, and scalability. Use PROACTIVELY when creating new services, designing APIs, modeling data, or evaluating architecture trade-offs. ([tontojil/PerfeckCode](https://github.com/tontojil/PerfeckCode/blob/HEAD/opencode-v2/agents/arquitecto-backend.md) · ★1)
- **arquitecto-backend**: Disena APIs, esquemas de base de datos y arquitectura backend escalable. Backend architect for API design, database schemas, microservice boundaries, and scalability. Use PROACTIVELY when creating new services, designing APIs, modeling data, or evaluating architecture trade-offs. ([tontojil/PerfeckCode](https://github.com/tontojil/PerfeckCode/blob/HEAD/opencode-v1/agents/arquitecto-backend.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **arquitecto-capas**: Validador de la arquitectura de capas del backoffice. Úsalo cuando se crea/modifica un feature o se duda de la dirección de dependencias y el foldering. Verifica el flujo Componente→Facade→Repository→DataSource, ausencia de ciclos y deps misma-capa, barrels y aliases. Read-only + madge. ([DanielSanR/PortalMisiones](https://github.com/DanielSanR/PortalMisiones/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arquitecto-capas.md))
- **arquitecto-dominio**: Guardián del modelo de dominio y de las fronteras hexagonales. Úsalo para diseñar o modificar agregados, objetos de valor, eventos y reglas de negocio del núcleo (Attendance, Compliance), para revisar que una implementación respeta la arquitectura, o cuando haya que decidir en qué módulo y en qué capa vive algo nuevo. Invócalo ANTES de escribir código de negocio, no después. ([BorjaPeonSaiz/KronoQR](https://github.com/BorjaPeonSaiz/KronoQR/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arquitecto-dominio.md))
- **arquitecto-rust**: Úsalo para diseñar o cambiar el dominio (crates/dominio), la persistencia SQLite (crates/almacen), el manejo de errores o el rendimiento. ([CpuJP/sueldapp](https://github.com/CpuJP/sueldapp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arquitecto-rust.md) · ★1)
- **arquitecto.agent**: Use when: designing architecture, evaluating patterns, making technical decisions, or reviewing code structure. Clean architecture, hexagonal, ADRs, production audit, coding standards. ([BUSCADO-LA-VIDA/Agents_IA_TECH](https://github.com/BUSCADO-LA-VIDA/Agents_IA_TECH/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/arquitecto.agent.md) · ★1)
- **arquiteto**: Cria e valida skills ausentes, gera design.md com diagrama Mermaid e tasks.md. Para e aguarda aprovação. Após aprovação, para e informa à thread principal quais agentes acionar — preferencialmente dev-fullstack por feature em paralelo. Não invoca outros agentes. ([marquesmarcelo/ia-dev-team](https://github.com/marquesmarcelo/ia-dev-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arquiteto.md) · ★4)
- **arquiteto**: Dono do contrato. Você conversa com o usuário, investiga o workspace e escreve `spec.md` e `plan.md`. O Maestro rege a execução do que você escreveu — ele não reescreve o contrato, e você não orquestra o time. `&lt;Maestro&gt;` é quem assinou a linha de *ask back* da sua última delegação. Recebeu aviso de troca de Maestro: passe a responder ao novo e reenvie o que ficou sem resposta. ([AndreRenatoMenezes/SDD-Maestri](https://github.com/AndreRenatoMenezes/SDD-Maestri/blob/HEAD/skills/sdd-maestri/agents/arquiteto.md) · ★3)
- **arquiteto**: Arquiteto de software. Use para desenhar e manter os diagramas de arquitetura do sistema (modelo C4 — contexto, contêineres e implantação) a partir do que existe no repositório (infra como código, manifestos, ADRs e specs), e para responder "como as peças se ligam" com o desenho. É chamado também quando a arquitetura mudou (o Stop cobra). Não use para escrever código, ADR, spec ou IaC (sessão principal), para tela (design) nem para decisão de produto (produto). ([JoaoCampista/sdlc-template](https://github.com/JoaoCampista/sdlc-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arquiteto.md) · ★3)
- **arquiteto**: Fable 5.1, o mais forte e o mais caro. Decisões difíceis em que errar custa caro: arquitetura, estratégia, escolher entre caminhos com trade-offs reais. Usar pouco, só quando o problema merece o melhor pensamento disponível. ([johnnyhelder/claude-model-super-power](https://github.com/johnnyhelder/claude-model-super-power/blob/HEAD/agents/arquiteto.md) · ★3)
- **arquiteto**: Especialista em arquitetura de sistemas, elaboração de escopo, governança técnica e validação contínua de etapas. ([Andre-Atlas/SUSANNA-HEALTHCHECK](https://github.com/Andre-Atlas/SUSANNA-HEALTHCHECK/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/arquiteto.md) · ★1)
- **arquiteto**: Coordenador único do projeto multi-agente. Ponto único de contato com o usuário humano. Decide, especifica, despacha trabalho para especialistas, integra os resultados e mantém a memória institucional do projeto viva. Use quando: o usuário quer planejar, decidir ou supervisionar trabalho; qualquer interação inicial com o sistema multi-agente passa por aqui. - **arquiteto**: Decide arquitetura, escopo e contratos técnicos a partir do briefing e das evidências do explorador; sintetiza o debate de ideias em apostas. - **arquiteto**: Use este agente no INÍCIO de toda feature ou mudança estrutural do HomeFinance — para planejar escopo, definir contratos de API, quebrar o trabalho em tarefas para os outros agentes e registrar decisões de arquitetura. - **arquiteto**: Decide stack, estrutura, contratos, estratégia de teste e de deploy — cada decisão com a justificativa junto. Papel da fase PLANO do ENGINE. Não escreve código de produção. - **arquiteto**: Use PROATIVAMENTE antes de implementar uma funcionalidade grande, criar um módulo ou serviço novo, reorganizar camadas, escolher entre monólito e serviços ou quando o usuário pedir "como estruturar", "qual arquitetura", "onde fica essa regra" ou trade-offs de design. - **arquiteto**: Arquiteto do odin. - **article-cover-generator**: SEO Phase 3 step. Runs ONLY when cover.enabled is true — otherwise exits SKIPPED. Generates ONE cover image automatically (OpenAI gpt-image-1), converts it to cover.webp in the article dir, and records the prompt into meta.json (cover.prompt). Project-agnostic; style hints from cover.style. Graceful skip when OPENAI_API_KEY is absent. - **article-critic**: Adversarially reviews a technical article draft on two axes, whether it is technically correct and whether it reads like a professional tech writer or like AI-generated slop. Verifies claims against primary sources, checks specifics against the assignment brief, runs a mechanical scan for AI tells, and returns PUBLISH, REVISE, or REWRITE with quoted, line-level findings. Use after tech-writer, or whenever the user asks to review, critique, or judge an article draft. Does not rewrite the article. ([1Shubham7/agents](https://github.com/1Shubham7/agents/blob/HEAD/agents/article-critic.md) · ★2)

- **article-domain-reviewer**: 記事の専門領域を検出し、最大3つの専門家ペルソナで公式事実・チーム運用・筆者解釈の境界を一次情報に照らしてreview-onlyで確認する。 ([s977043/my-blog](https://github.com/s977043/my-blog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-domain-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **article-draft-writer**: SEO Phase 3 step. Writes the full article draft from brief + outline + sources, in the project's language and voice. Writes human-sounding prose (voice, burstiness, specifics) — not smooth AI filler. ([alexfilatov/seo-suite](https://github.com/alexfilatov/seo-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/article-draft-writer.md) · ★1)

- **article-fact-checker**: Step 6/9 of SEO Phase 3 — fact-check gate. Extracts every hard fact / concrete claim from the draft and checks it against sources.json, driven by the project profile's contentRigor. In regulated mode it is a HARD gate (missing/off-whitelist citation = BLOCK); in standard mode it is a LIGHT gate (concrete claims need a named credible source; soft issues = WARN). Output: &lt;articlesDir&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;/fact-check.json. ([alexfilatov/seo-suite](https://github.com/alexfilatov/seo-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/article-fact-checker.md) · ★1)
- **article-fetcher**: Fetch a single article from a URL and save it to the media-monitoring archive following date-based folder conventions and the article schema. Use when the user provides an article URL to archive, asks to grab a piece of coverage, or wants to capture an article referenced in conversation. - **article-html-author**: 確定済み記事 Markdown とカバー画像から html-page-design 準拠のリッチ HTML 記事ページと記事一覧トップ index.html を執筆し、公開元ディレクトリへ出力する。全工程合格時のみ commit→push で GitHub Pages へ公開する。レビューレポートを受け取った場合は /multi-aspect-review の再生成 author として該当 HTML を上書き再執筆する（再生成時は push しない）。本文の事実・主張は改変しない。 ([TomokiIshimine/paper-lens](https://github.com/TomokiIshimine/paper-lens/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-html-author.md) · ★1)

- **article-illustrator**: [Subagent] 文章配图师。 负责为文章设计视觉风格，自动生成配图并插入到文章中。 采用 "Type × Style" 双维度设计理论，确保配图既有信息量又有美感。 ([railwise-cn/railwise-ai](https://github.com/railwise-cn/railwise-ai/blob/HEAD/src/asset/skills/writing-agent/agents/article-illustrator.md) · ★3)
- **article-image-generator**: 確定済み記事本文からインフォグラフィック 1 枚の構図・配色・画像内日本語文言を設計し、codex-imagegen スキル経由で PNG を生成・保存して絶対パスを返す。レビューレポートを受け取った場合は /multi-aspect-review の再生成 author としてcover.png を上書き再生成する。article.md は書き換えない。 ([TomokiIshimine/paper-lens](https://github.com/TomokiIshimine/paper-lens/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-image-generator.md) · ★1)
- **article-legal-reviewer**: SEO Phase 3 compliance gate. Runs ONLY when contentRigor === "regulated" AND regulated.legalReview is true — otherwise exits SKIPPED. Verifies the article carries the required disclaimer, grounds the expected key acts, and contains no forbidden product claims. Project-agnostic; all criteria come from the regulated.* block of seo/profile.json. Records its verdict into meta.json. ([alexfilatov/seo-suite](https://github.com/alexfilatov/seo-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/article-legal-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **article-linker**: 内部リンク最適化専門エージェント。以下のタスクで使用する： - article-index.json から last_linked が古い/null の記事を最大10件バッチ処理 - 双方向リンク：対象記事→関連記事 と 関連記事→対象記事 の両方を追加 - article-link-card 形式でH2セクション末尾に挿入（常に最良の5本を維持） - 5本上限到達時は新候補と既存リンクの関連度を比較し、より高いものに入れ替える - article-index.json の existing_internal_links / internal_link_count / last_linked を更新 ([gucchi0421/.claude](https://github.com/gucchi0421/.claude/blob/HEAD/__BK__/agents/article-linker.md))
